[48] browser_evaluate  input={"function": "() => { const el = document.querySelector('input[placeholder=\"What are you selling?\"]'); return {tag: el.tagName, required: el.required, validat
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
### Result
{
  "tag": "INPUT",
  "required": true,
  "validationMessage": "Please fill out this field.",
  "willValidate": true
}
### Ran Playwright code
```js
await page.evaluate('() => { const el = document.querySelector(\'input[placeholder="What are you selling?"]\'); return {tag: el.tagName, required: el.required, validationMessage: el.validationMessage, willValidate: el.willValidate}; }');
```
